Separate code intent, measured geometry/render metadata, actual successful execution and visual quality. For multi-object/part connections, code coordinates or AABBs do not prove physical contact or image fidelity. Do not treat an available teacher policy as received, or numeric acceptance as independently validated quality. Distinguish technical rendering failures from completed but potentially poor reconstruction. No new code or skill updates.

00062: def rugflower(name,x,y,r,col):
00063:     nodes=[]
00064:     for j in range(7):
00065:         a=j*2*math.pi/7
00066:         p=ball(r*.46, center=(x+math.cos(a)*r*.55,y+math.sin(a)*r*.55,.034), color=col)
00067:         p.resize((1,.55,.045))
00068:         p.turn('z',math.degrees(a))
00069:         nodes.append(p)
00070:     p=ball(r*.23, center=(x,y,.037), color=(.53,.31,.16))
00071:     p.resize((1,1,.08))
00072:     nodes.append(p)
00073:     scene.include(name,assemble(nodes))

00176: def curtain(name,x0,x1,y,z0,z1,color,translucent=False):
00177:     verts=[]
00178:     faces=[]
00179:     count=72

00182:         x=x0+(x1-x0)*t

00184:         verts.extend([(x,yy,z0),(x,yy,z1)])
00185:     for i in range(count):
00186:         faces.append((2*i,2*i+2,2*i+3,2*i+1))
00187:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00188:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces)
